Ask when what. The alarm sounds. Under this rule, that event guarantees evacuation. Whenever makes the same repeated-case meaning explicit. But When I checked the desk, a notice was there reports one occasion; it doesn't promise a notice every time I check.\"\n+group1[3] = \"Where can make a rule about a location or situation: Where a station is available, the service operates. Which locations does the rule cover? Locations with an available station. At one of those locations, the service has to operate. Keep the location connection when you read the rule back; a station elsewhere hasn't met this location's condition.\"\n+group1[4] = \"All covers the whole group it names: All registered guests receive a wristband. All what? Registered guests, not guests generally. Being one of those registered guests is enough for a wristband. Another guest may receive one too; the sentence hasn't told us. It also hasn't told us that a registered guest exists.\"\n+group1[5] = \"The only needs careful reading: The only vehicles permitted inside are electric vehicles. The only what? Vehicles permitted inside. If a vehicle is permitted inside, it must be electric. Try electric vehicle → permitted inside. That would grant permission to every electric vehicle, which the sentence never promised. The grammatical target is the whole permission description, not the word electric.\"\n+group1[6] = \"Every takes the members one at a time but covers them all: Every finalist receives an invitation. Choose a finalist. That person has to receive an invitation under the rule. All finalists receive an invitation would express the same relationship. Neither wording says everyone receiving an invitation is a finalist.\"\n+group1[7] = \"Any says that whichever member of the named group you choose, the rule applies: Any applicant who submits late pays a fee. Choose a late-submitting applicant and that person pays a fee under the rule. Choose an applicant who submits on time and this sentence doesn't settle the fee question. Submits late stays with applicant in your diagram.\"\n+draft['units']['group-1']['method'][1] = \"Name both complete ideas. Keep who or what they concern, as well as restrictions such as registered or submits late.\"\n+\n+group2 = draft['units']['group-2']['teaching']\n+group2[3] = \"Only if introduces a condition that has to be met: A student graduates only if the student completes the project. If the student graduates, the project must be completed. Project completion by itself doesn't promise graduation. Read the reverse aloud and you'll hear the extra guarantee it adds.\"\n+group2[4] = \"Only when makes an event or state required: A review occurs only when a complaint is filed. A complaint has to be filed for this review to occur. Imagine a complaint has been filed. Does this sentence say a review now occurs? No. It says what a review requires, not that every complaint gets one.\"\n+group2[5] = \"Only where makes a location condition required: The service operates only where a station is available. Wherever the service operates, a station has to be available there. A station at another location isn't enough. Nor has the sentence promised service to every place with an available station.\"\n+group2[6] = \"Always describes the result that holds throughout the named cases: A finalist always receives an invitation. Start with a finalist. Under that claim, the person receives an invitation. The subject supplies the sufficient idea; receiving an invitation is the required result. Always belongs in this teaching group, but it doesn't mean the next word automatically goes on the right. Read the complete subject and result.\"\n+group2[7] = \"Requires tells you what is needed: Entry requires a current pass. Requires what? A current pass. Entry goes left, having the current pass goes right. Change the wording to A current pass is required for entry. The requirement appears first now, but the relationship hasn't changed. A pass alone isn't a promise that you can enter, and an expired pass doesn't meet the full requirement.\"\n+group2[10] = \"Keep every part of a requirement. In Entry requires a current pass, pass would drop current. In Only members may use this room, actually uses the room would change permission into an action. The right direction can't repair a label that has lost part of the idea.\"\n+\n+units = {}\n+for unit in baseline:\n+    if unit['id'] not in opening_ids:\n+        continue\n+    unit.update(draft['units'][unit['id']])\n+    for question in [unit['guidedExample'], *(question for level in ['beginner', 'medium', 'advanced'] for question in unit['drills'][level])]:\n+        hint, steps = draft['questionFeedback'][question['id']]\n+        question.update(hint=hint, reasoningSteps=steps, explanation=' '.join(steps))\n+        question['id'] = replacements.get(question['id'], question['id'])\n+    units[unit['id']] = unit\n+\n+def js(value):\n+    return json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False)\n+\n+def lines(values, indent=6):\n+    return '[\\r\\n' + ''.join(' ' * indent + js(value) + ',\\r\\n' for value in values) + ' ' * (indent - 2) + ']'\n+\n+def question_call(question):\n+    return 'question(' + ', '.join(js(question[key]) for key in ['id', 'prompt', 'options', 'answerIndex', 'hint', 'reasoningSteps']) + ')'\n+\n+def source_unit(unit):\n+    text = '  {\\r\\n'\n+    text += f\"    id: '{unit['id']}', title: {js(unit['title'])}, area: '{unit['area']}', phase: '{unit['phase']}', prerequisites: {js(unit['prerequisites'])},\\r\\n\"\n+    text += f\"    goal: {js(unit['goal'])},\\r\\n\"\n+    for field in ['teaching', 'method']:\n+        text += f'    {field}: ' + lines(unit[field]) + ',\\r\\n'\n+    worked = unit['workedExample']\n+    text += '    workedExample: {\\r\\n'\n+    for field in ['statements', 'reasoning', 'conclusion']:\n+        text += f'      {field}: {js(worked[field])},\\r\\n'\n+    text += '    },\\r\\n'\n+    text += '    guidedExample: ' + question_call(unit['guidedExample']) + ',\\r\\n'\n+    text += '    commonMistake: ' + '{ ' + ', '.join(f'{key}: {js(value)}' for key, value in unit['commonMistake'].items()) + ' },\\r\\n'\n+    text += '    drills: {\\r\\n'\n+    for level in ['beginner', 'medium', 'advanced']:\n+        text += f'      {level}: [\\r\\n'\n+        text += ''.join('        ' + question_call(question) + ',\\r\\n' for question in unit['drills'][level])\n+        text += '      ],\\r\\n'\n+    return text + '    },\\r\\n  },\\r\\n'\n+\n+source_path = repo / 'src/bootcamps/conditionals/content/foundations.source.ts'\n+source = source_path.read_bytes().decode('utf-8')\n+matches = list(re.finditer(r\"(?m)^  \\{\\r?\\n    id: '([^']+)'\", source))\n+patches = []\n+for index, match in enumerate(matches):\n+    unit_id = match.group(1)\n+    if unit_id in units:\n+        patches.append((match.start(), matches[index + 1].start(), source_unit(units[unit_id])))\n+assert len(patches) == 4\n+original_other_blocks = {match.group(1): source[match.start():matches[index + 1].start()] for index, match in enumerate(matches[:-1]) if match.group(1) not in opening_ids}\n+for start, end, replacement in reversed(patches):\n+    source = source[:start] + replacement + source[end:]\n+for original in original_other_blocks.values():\n+    assert original in source\n+source_path.write_bytes(source.encode('utf-8'))\n+\n+historical = json.loads((proof / 'historical-questions.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))\n+legacy = \"import type { ConditionalDifficulty, ConditionalQuestion } from './types';\\n\\n\"\n+legacy += 'export interface LegacyConditionalQuestion extends ConditionalQuestion {\\n  unitId: string;\\n  unitTitle: string;\\n  difficulty: ConditionalDifficulty;\\n}\\n\\n'\n+legacy += '// Original questions remain available only to saved runs and attempt review.\\n// They require later inference and must never enter newly assigned practice.\\n'\n+legacy += 'const historicalQuestions: LegacyConditionalQuestion[] = ' + json.dumps(historical, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + ';\\n\\n'\n+legacy += 'export function findLegacyConditionalQuestion(id: string): LegacyConditionalQuestion | undefined {\\n  return historicalQuestions.find(question => question.id === id);\\n}\\n'\n+(repo / 'src/bootcamps/conditionals/content/legacyQuestions.ts').write_text(legacy, encoding='utf-8', newline='\\n')\n+\n+# Update the concrete review draft to the authorized, actually applied scope.\n+draft_text = re.sub(r'```json\\n.*?\\n```', '```json\\n' + json.dumps(draft, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + '\\n```', draft_text, flags=re.S)\n+draft_text = draft_text.replace('App authoring is paused pending the actual Claude plan review. This file is an external, concrete proposal; it does not modify the app.', 'Applied to the four owned units after the coordinating agent authorized implementation under Joshua\\'s build request. Claude plan round 2 returned REVISE; the host accepted the warranted corrections. Final actual Claude inspection is still required.')\n+draft_text = draft_text.replace('The existing optional wording extensions stay available with their limits.', 'The optional wording extensions are removed following the actual Claude scope review. The fourteen core expressions remain developed individually.')\n+draft_text = draft_text.replace('Nothing here teaches contraposition, combining rules, Groups 3/4 or quantities.', 'The newly authored opening teaches no contraposition, combining rules, Groups 3/4 or quantities. Historical questions remain outside new practice.')\n+draft_path.write_text(draft_text, encoding='utf-8', newline='\\n')\n+\n+projection = [(unit['id'], level, question['id'], question['prompt'], question['options'], question['answerIndex']) for unit_id in opening_ids for unit in [units[unit_id]] for level in ['beginner', 'medium', 'advanced'] for question in unit['drills'][level]]\n+signature = hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(projection, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(',', ':')).encode('utf-8')).hexdigest()\n+print(json.dumps({'openingPracticeCount': len(projection), 'practiceContractSha256': signature, 'historicalSha256': hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(historical, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(',', ':')).encode('utf-8')).hexdigest(), 'renamed': replacements}, indent=2))\n*** End Patch"));

Only the three already-replaced\n+    // questions receive -v2 IDs; level, order, prompt, options and key stay fixed.\n+    const contract = openingIds.flatMap(unitId => {\n+      const unit = opening.find(item => item.id === unitId)!;\n+      return levels.flatMap(level => unit.drills[level].map(question => [\n+        unit.id, level, question.id, question.prompt, question.options, question.answerIndex,\n+      ]));\n+    });\n+    expect(contract).toHaveLength(62);\n+    expect(fingerprint(contract)).toBe('c1c5618af93266eb86b54ad22e12cdc9bb5b93b584dd7b8ce4bfb95e5ef2b30a');\n+    const newPracticeIds = opening.flatMap(unit => levels.flatMap(level => unit.drills[level].map(question => question.id)));\n+    for (const id of historicalIds) {\n+      expect(newPracticeIds).not.toContain(id);\n+      expect(newPracticeIds).toContain(`${id}-v2`);\n+    }\n+  });\n+\n+  it('retains the exact historical questions for old saved work without offering them in new practice', () => {\n+    const originals = historicalIds.map(id => findLegacyConditionalQuestion(id));\n+    expect(originals.every(Boolean)).toBe(true);\n+    // Includes historical hint and worked reasoning, as well as each original\n+    // prompt and numeric answer contract. No machine-specific fixture is needed.\n+    expect(fingerprint(originals)).toBe('def6ed0dc3e0e5aa32ac8b3a14ca4e97c1b261cd9764d2ea99096a035e5a47f8');\n+    expect(findLegacyConditionalQuestion('group-1-a3-v2')).toBeUndefined();\n+    expect(findLegacyConditionalQuestion('orientation-b1')).toBeUndefined();\n+  });\n*** End Patch"));

createHash } from 'node:crypto';\n+import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';\n+import { foundationUnits } from '../../../../../ai-os-projects/lsatu-conditional-bootcamp/src/bootcamps/conditionals/content/foundations.source';\n+import { applicationUnits } from '../../../../../ai-os-projects/lsatu-conditional-bootcamp/src/bootcamps/conditionals/content/applications.source';\n+import { validateConditionalContent } from '../../../../../ai-os-projects/lsatu-conditional-bootcamp/src/bootcamps/conditionals/content/validate';\n+import { findLegacyConditionalQuestion } from '../../../../../ai-os-projects/lsatu-conditional-bootcamp/src/bootcamps/conditionals/content/legacyQuestions';\n+\n+const openingIds = ['orientation', 'sufficient-necessary', 'group-1', 'group-2'];\n+const levels = ['beginner', 'medium', 'advanced'] as const;\n+const replacements: Record<string, string> = { 'group-1-a3': 'group-1-a3-v2', 'group-2-m2': 'group-2-m2-v2', 'group-2-a3': 'group-2-a3-v2' };\n+const baseline = JSON.parse(readFileSync(new URL('./curriculum-before.json', import.meta.url), 'utf8'));\n+const historical = JSON.parse(readFileSync(new URL('./historical-questions.json', import.meta.url), 'utf8'));\n+const units = [...foundationUnits, ...applicationUnits];\n+const errors = validateConditionalContent(units);\n+if (errors.length) throw new Error(errors.join('\\n'));\n+const changedLater = baseline.filter(unit => !openingIds.includes(unit.id)).filter(unit => JSON.stringify(unit) !== JSON.stringify(units.find(current => current.id === unit.id))).map(unit => unit.id);\n+if (changedLater.length) throw new Error(`Later content changed: ${changedLater.join(', ')}`);\n+const project = (curriculum, mapIds = false) => openingIds.flatMap(id => {\n+  const unit = curriculum.find(item => item.id === id);\n+  return levels.flatMap(level => unit.drills[level].map(question => [unit.id, level, mapIds ? replacements[question.id] ?? question.id : question.id, question.prompt, question.options, question.answerIndex]));\n+});\n+const expected = project(baseline, true);\n+const actual = project(units);\n+if (JSON.stringify(expected) !== JSON.stringify(actual)) throw new Error('Opening practice contract changed.');\n+const expectedGuides = baseline.filter(unit => openingIds.includes(unit.id)).map(unit => [unit.id, unit.guidedExample.id, unit.guidedExample.prompt, unit.guidedExample.options, unit.guidedExample.answerIndex]);\n+const actualGuides = units.filter(unit => openingIds.includes(unit.id)).map(unit => [unit.id, unit.guidedExample.id, unit.guidedExample.prompt, unit.guidedExample.options, unit.guidedExample.answerIndex]);\n+if (JSON.stringify(expectedGuides) !== JSON.stringify(actualGuides)) throw new Error('Guided answer contract changed.');\n+for (const row of historical) if (JSON.stringify(findLegacyConditionalQuestion(row.id)) !== JSON.stringify(row)) throw new Error(`Historical question changed: ${row.id}`);\n+const hash = value => createHash('sha256').update(JSON.stringify(value)).digest('hex');\n+console.log(JSON.stringify({ units: units.length, totalPractice: units.flatMap(unit => Object.values(unit.drills).flat()).length, Avoid unsupported prevalence statistics.\\r\\n3. **Keep one relationship stable while changing the view.** Ordinary sentence, case fact, roles, wording variation, and diagram should fit together.\\r\\n4. **Make each pause a useful thinking task.** Ask for a prediction or explanation, then reveal the reason.\\r\\n5. **Show a mistake being detected.** A wrong arrow becomes understandable when its English meaning fails the original sentence.\\r\\n6. **Teach the target before the placement shortcut becomes a habit.** Complete ideas and their qualifications matter.\\r\\n7. **Explain why the drill exists.** The learner should know what ability they are practicing.\\r\\n8. **Use fresh practice.** Worked examples teach the method; new items show whether the method transfers.\\r\\n9. **Give practical objections an answer.** Time, confidence, notation, and when to write deserve specific guidance.\\r\\n10. **Keep later content in its proper lesson.** Contraposition, chains, Groups 3/4, and quantities receive their own development; a quality reference does not override prerequisites.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Accuracy and method boundaries\\r\\n\\r\\nWriting that sounds like the source must still preserve correct logic and Joshua\'s chosen method.\\r\\n\\r\\n| Source simplification or context | Boundary for our material |\\r\\n|---|---|\\r\\n| “Ignore the conditional” when sufficient is absent or necessary is present. | For this one rule and fact, no new conclusion about the other condition follows. The rule remains available for later facts and chains. Never teach that it disappears. |\\r\\n| Necessary has to happen “at some point.” | A conditional is not necessarily a time sequence or causal claim. Prefer “has to be true if the sufficient condition is true.” |\\r\\n| The original and contrapositive are “all you know.” | The central warning is against an unsupported reverse or inverse. Equivalent formulations and other supplied rules still matter. Diagram the stated requirement unless the facts establish compliance. |\\r\\n| Indicator usually comes before target. | Read its full grammatical target. Targets can precede the indicator, and familiar words can serve other roles in context. |\\r\\n| Shorten conditions to one or two letters, never more than four. | Joshua\'s clear condensed labels govern. Keep negation, scope, qualifiers, and readability. A short label that loses meaning is not an improvement. |\\r\\n| Try a personalized sufficient guess. | Compare with the whole ordinary meaning and look for a counterexample. A feeling that an arrow is plausible is not enough. |\\r\\n| Original and contrapositive training. | Explain why a drill requires explicit work and when test-day practice may differ. Do not turn a training requirement into a universal performance rule. |\\r\\n| “Always” is in a necessary-family teaching list. | Establish the sentence\'s subject and relationship. It is not a universal instruction to place the next word on the right. |\\r\\n| Confidence guarantees the diagram is correct. | Confidence can grow from practice, but correctness rests on preserving meaning and valid reasoning. Provide checks. |\\r\\n| Timing and learning claims are absolute. | Treat them as the author\'s advice unless independently supported. reverse:\\r\\n\\r\\n- Blue sticker and inspected: fits the rule.\\r\\n- Inspected without a blue sticker: also fits the rule.\\r\\n- Blue sticker without inspection: breaks the rule.\\r\\n\\r\\n"}}'}, indicator groups\', () => {\\r\\n    expect(conditionalUnits.slice(0, 4).map(unit => unit.id)).toEqual(openingIds);\\r\\n    expect(opening.map(unit => unit.prerequisites)).toEqual([\\r\\n      [], [\'orientation\'], [\'sufficient-necessary\'], [\'group-1\'],\\r\\n    ]);\\r\\n  });\\r\\n\\r\\n  it(\'keeps new explanations and practice in the shipped reference bundle\', () => {\\r\\n    for (const unit of opening) {\\r\\n      expect(unit).toEqual(foundationUnits.find(source => source.id === unit.id));\\r\\n      expect(JSON.stringify(unit)).not.toMatch(/\\\\b(?:dogs?|mammals?)\\\\b/i);\\r\\n      expect(unit.method.join(\' \')).not.toMatch(/contrapositive|contraposition|chain|quantity/i);\\r\\n      expect(unit.teaching.join(\' \')).not.toMatch(/\\\\b(?:contrapositive|contraposition|chains?|quantifiers?|quantities)\\\\b/i);\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  });\\r\\n\\r\\n  it(\'preserves the 62-question answer contract used by saved choices and diagrams\', () => {\\r\\n    // Captured before the 2026-10-02 voice rewrite. Only the three already-replaced\\r\\n    // questions receive -v2 IDs; level, order, prompt, options and key stay fixed.\\r\\n    const contract = openingIds.flatMap(unitId => {\\r\\n      const unit = opening.find(item => item.id === unitId)!;\\r\\n      return levels.flatMap(level => unit.drills[level].map(question => [\\r\\n        unit.id, level, question.id, question.prompt, question.options, question.answerIndex,\\r\\n      ]));\\r\\n    });\\r\\n    expect(contract).toHaveLength(62);\\r\\n    expect(fingerprint(contract)).toBe(\'c1c5618af93266eb86b54ad22e12cdc9bb5b93b584dd7b8ce4bfb95e5ef2b30a\');\\r\\n    const newPracticeIds = opening.flatMap(unit => levels.flatMap(level => unit.drills[level].map(question => question.id)));\\r\\n    for (const id of historicalIds) {\\r\\n      expect(newPracticeIds).not.toContain(id);\\r\\n      expect(newPracticeIds).toContain(`${id}-v2`);\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  });\\r\\n\\r\\n  it(\'retains the exact historical questions for old saved work without offering them in new practice\', () => {\\r\\n    const originals = historicalIds.map(id => findLegacyConditionalQuestion(id));\\r\\n    expect(originals.every(Boolean)).toBe(true);\\r\\n    // Includes historical hint and worked reasoning, as well as each original\\r\\n    // prompt and numeric answer contract. No machine-specific fixture is needed.\\r\\n    expect(fingerprint(originals)).toBe(\'def6ed0dc3e0e5aa32ac8b3a14ca4e97c1b261cd9764d2ea99096a035e5a47f8\');\\r\\n    expect(findLegacyConditionalQuestion(\'group-1-a3-v2\')).toBeUndefined();\\r\\n    expect(findLegacyConditionalQuestion(\'orientation-b1\')).toBeUndefined();\\r\\n  });\\r\\n\\r\\n  const families = [\\r\\n    {\\r\\n      unitId: \'group-1\',\\r\\n      indicators: [/\\\\bif\\\\b/i, /\\\\bwhen(?:ever)?\\\\b/i, /\\\\bwhere\\\\b/i, /\\\\ball\\\\b/i, /\\\\bthe only\\\\b/i, /\\\\bevery\\\\b/i, /\\\\bany\\\\b/i],\\r\\n    },\\r\\n    {\\r\\n      unitId: \'group-2\',\\r\\n      indicators: [/\\\\bonly (?!if\\\\b|when\\\\b|where\\\\b)/i, /\\\\bonly if\\\\b/i, /\\\\bonly when\\\\b/i, /\\\\bonly where\\\\b/i, /\\\\balways\\\\b/i, /\\\\brequires?\\\\b|\\\\brequired\\\\b/i, /\\\\bmust\\\\b/i],\\r\\n    },\\r\\n  ];\\r\\n\\r\\n  for (const { unitId, indicators } of families) {\\r\\n    for (const level of levels) {\\r\\n      it(`${unitId} ${level} practices every core indicator in a conditional claim`, () => {\\r\\n        const unit = opening.find(item => item.id === unitId)!;\\r\\n        // Inquiry/event discrimination is useful, but cannot replace practicing a rule.\\r\\n        const rulePractice = unit.drills[level].filter(item =>\\r\\n          !/reports an inquiry|reports one visit/i.test(item.options[item.answerIndex])\\r\\n        );\\r\\n        for (const indicator of indicators) {\\r\\n          expect(rulePractice.some(item => indicator.test(item.prompt)), `${indicator} has no conditional practice`).toBe(true);\\r\\n        }\\r\\n      });\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  it(\'teaches must as an obligation at every level without asserting compliance\', () => {\\r\\n"}}'}]

first. The opening lessons apply it closely, and the round-1 corrections hold up. Orientation defines a condition and a conditional statement before it shows an example. Sufficient and necessary are taught as separate steps on the same ready/packed rule. Prediction questions really do pause before the answer is revealed. \'Must\' stays an obligation everywhere. Wrong answers in the in-lesson checks and diagram builder get feedback specific to that choice. All 44 Group 1/2 practice items are logically correct. The four historical questions match HEAD exactly. Reconciling saved practice runs is limited to registered content changes and repeats safely. The scoped diff leaves out the bank, finder and MCP files.\\\\n\\\\nTwo medium defects remain:\\\\n- **Review-link date resets on every build.** The cut-off date for the \'Review expanded lesson\' link now comes from BUILD_TIME, which Vite regenerates on every build. Each later deploy, even an unrelated one, would show the link again to everyone who read the lesson before that deploy.\\\\n- **Two named methods are never taught.** The guided activities, the method checklist and the reference tell students to use a \'Personalized If/Then\' and the \'What Test\'. The opening lesson that students actually see never teaches either name, because the old lesson text that did is no longer shown for these four units.\\\\n\\\\nLow-severity issues:\\\\n- The \'Review the move\' link for the four opening lessons points to a #repair section that the new lesson doesn\'t have.\\\\n- The orientation lesson\'s title changed, so the \'Level N\' wording is not removed from its existing assignment titles. Classroom pages outside the bootcamp also still show the stored \'Level N\' titles.\\\\n- Repeated \'waiting for a ready label\' wording suggests a timeline, which the lesson itself says the arrow does not mean.\\\\n- Optional: one weak distractor, and two stock phrases (\'under this rule\', \'hasn\'t been made enough\') repeated 47 times.\\",\\r\\n    \\"findings\\": [\\r\\n      {\\r\\n        \\"id\\": \\"FI-1\\",\\r\\n        \\"severity\\": \\"medium\\",\\r\\n        \\"path\\": \\"src/bootcamps/conditionals/openingRevision.ts\\",\\r\\n        \\"evidence\\": \\"Line 14 sets CONDITIONAL_OPENING_REVISED_AT from BUILD_TIME. vite.config.ts:19 sets BUILD_TIME = new Date().toISOString() on every build and injects it through define (line 37). No VITE_CONDITIONAL_OPENING_RELEASED_AT is set in any env file; the only reference is in openingRevision.ts. needsExpandedConditionalLessonReview (line 16) returns true whenever lessons[unit.id] is earlier than the cut-off. Failure scenario: the expanded lesson ships on build T1, and a student reads it at T1+1 day. A later unrelated deploy at T2 moves the cut-off to T2. ExpandedLessonReview (routes.tsx:202-210) then shows \'Review expanded lesson\' to that student, who already read the expanded lesson. This happens again after every deploy. progress.test.ts:51 asserts CONDITIONAL_OPENING_REVISED_AT === BUILD_TIME, so the tests lock in this behavior. The fix for OR-4 swapped \'too early\' for \'moves with every build\'. The R2-3 rule (show the link only to readers of the shorter lesson) is not met in production.\\",\\r\\n        \\"fix\\": \\"Pin the cut-off to one fixed publication time. Either hard-code an ISO date in openingRevision.ts at release, or require VITE_CONDITIONAL_OPENING_RELEASED_AT in a committed .env.production and fail the production build when it is missing or invalid. Use BUILD_TIME only in dev/test, never in production. Replace the progress.test assertion with one that the production cut-off is a fixed literal value that does not depend on the build.\\"\\r\\n      },\\r\\n      {\\r\\n        \\"id\\": \\"FI-2\\",\\r\\n        \\"severity\\": \\"medium\\",\\r\\n        \\"path\\": \\"src/bootcamps/conditionals/content/foundations.source.ts\\",\\r\\n        \\"evidence\\": \\"ConditionalLessonNote.tsx:26 sends all four opening units to ConditionalOpeningLesson, which never renders unit.teaching. The rendered lesson (ConditionalOpeningLesson.tsx) never mentions it (grep finds no \'Personalized\' in components). Yet the next recorded step tells students to use it. sufficient-necessary-guide (line 129) says: \'Before choosing, use a Personalized If/Then\'. group-2-guide (line 333) and the Group 2 method (line 325) say the same. The Group 1/2 method steps (lines 259, 322) name the \'What Test\', which the rendered lesson practices (\'Add what to the indicator\') but never names. These method lists appear in the guided activity\'s \'Build your response in these steps\' (routes.tsx:272), in the reference dialog (routes.tsx:168) and on the Reference page. The guide\'s review step asks: \'Does each new term refer to an idea they have seen?\' The plan also says recorded work must use only what was taught. This is the same kind of gap as OR-3.\\",\\r\\n        \\"fix\\": \\"Add a short rendered section to the sufficient-necessary lesson on reading a rule from the inside. In Group 1\'s \'Find the target, then place it\' section, name the \'What Test\' where \'If what? All what?\' is introduced. Alternatively, reword the guided prompts and method steps to describe the check without the untaught names. Add a test that every named method in an opening unit\'s method or guidedExample appears in the rendered lesson text.\\"\\r\\n      },\\r\\n      {\\r\\n        \\"id\\": \\"FI-3\\",\\r\\n        \\"severity\\": \\"low\\",\\r\\n        \\"path\\": \\"src/bootcamps/conditionals/routes.tsx\\",\\r\\n        \\"evidence\\": \\"UnitActions (line 157) shows \'4. The \'#repair\' section with commonMistake exists only in the legacy note (line 36). The opening component has no id=\'repair\' and does not show unit.commonMistake (grep finds no \'repair\' anchor). That no longer matches the rebuilt \'What Are Conditionals?: Level 1 …\', so the row keeps showing the level label and the old title. Stage titles (\'…start with Find the Guarantee\') keep the old name the same way. Separately, normalization runs only in resolveConditionalAssignments. Student classroom pages show the raw stored title, for example BootcampsSection.tsx:90 renders {it.title}. Existing rows therefore still say \'Level N\' outside the bootcamp. We\'re recording what follows logically, not a timeline.\' The guide\'s accuracy table also warns against reading a conditional as a time sequence. Yet the case where an order is packed but not ready is repeatedly described as waiting: SetExplorer button \'Could be waiting for a ready label\' (line 40), GateExplorer \'Packing might be complete before that label is added\' (line 45), \'packed and waiting for a ready label\' (697), \'D could be packed and waiting for its label\' (723), \'packed and waiting for its ready label\' (800). ConditionalOpeningMedia.tsx:15 and 74 say the same, and so does the teaching array. A packed order that is not marked ready need not ever get the label. Saying it is \'waiting\' quietly suggests every packed order ends up ready, which is the reverse the lesson is trying to rule out.\\",\\r\\n        \\"fix\\": \\"Describe the allowed case without a timeline: \'packed but not marked ready\', and label the button \'Could be packed without a ready label\'. The Check at line 750 asks \'Every finalist receives an invitation. What is necessary for being a finalist?\' with the options \'Receiving an invitation\' and \'Being the winner\'. The guide\'s \'Tempting reverse\' pattern calls for making the attractive wrong choice visible.\\",\\r\\n        \\"fix\\": \\"Replace \'Being the winner\' with a reversal-based distractor such as \'Being a finalist is necessary for an invitation\', and give it feedback that names the reversed requirement.\\"\\r\\n      },\\r\\n      {\\r\\n        \\"id\\": \\"FI-7\\",\\r\\n        \\"severity\\": \\"low\\",\\r\\n        \\"path\\": \\"src/bootcamps/conditionals/components/ConditionalOpeningLesson.tsx\\",\\r\\n        \\"evidence\\": \\"Optional voice issue. \'under this rule\' / \'under the rule\' / \'hasn\'t been made [enough|proof]\' / \'stated enough\' appear 29 times in ConditionalOpeningLesson.tsx and 18 times in foundations.source.ts. Examples: \'hasn\'t been made a promise of a key\', \'Membership hasn\'t been made enough\', \'A record alone hasn\'t been stated enough\'. The guide warns against \'a recurring catchphrase that sounds inserted\' and asks writers to \'Remove repeated catchphrases\'. Read aloud across 14 explanations and 44 drills, these become a stock phrase rather than reasoning, and the passive \'hasn\'t been made enough\' is stiffer than the guide\'s own examples (\'The rule hasn\'t answered that question\').\\",\\r\\n        \\"fix\\": \\"Vary the wording by what each sentence needs. Sandra\'s 2026-09-28 five-step method adds contraposition in a later lesson;\r\n * archived six-step notes divide concept identification and labeling differently.\r\n * Fact/rule contrasts follow Yahunatan\'s current V10 recap and Sofia\'s\r\n * 2026-09-11 Conditional MBT draft. Their local draft status is not proof of posting.\r\n * Recall and Rephrase instructions are an authored adaptation of archived homework\r\n * naming that exercise; the archive does not supply its complete original procedure.\r\n * Extended expression families also reference Asia\'s assigned 2026-08-24\r\n * Conditional Logic Cheat Sheet and The Breakdown; contextual meaning takes\r\n * precedence over their mechanical word-list shortcuts.\r\n * All scenarios, questions and explanations below are newly authored, not official LSAT items.\r\n * Difficulty concerns interpretation and inference. These sets prescribe no mastery threshold or clock.\r\n */\r\nconst question = (\r\n  id: string, prompt: string, options: string[], answerIndex: number,\r\n  hint: string, reasoningSteps: string[],\r\n): ConditionalQuestion => ({ id, prompt, options, answerIndex, hint, reasoningSteps, explanation: reasoningSteps.join(\' \') });\r\n\r\nexport const foundationUnits: ConditionalUnit[] = [\r\n  {\r\n    id: \'orientation\', title: "What Are Conditionals?", area: \'understanding\', phase: \'conditional\', prerequisites: [],\r\n    goal: "Explain what a conditional says, use it with a fact, and say what the information leaves open.",\r\n    teaching: [\r\n      "A conditional tells us that if one condition is true, another has to be true too. A condition is something we can say about a situation, such as an order being marked ready or its having been packed. The conditional connects those two ideas. It doesn\'t tell us that either one has happened.",\r\n      "Why learn this? A conclusion on the LSAT can depend on which way that connection runs. If you know what the rule promises, you can explain why a conclusion follows. If an answer uses the rule in the other direction, you can see the missing connection instead of accepting a conclusion that sounds reasonable.",\r\n      "Let\'s use one rule: Every order marked ready has been packed. Take that claim as true for this exercise. Every ready order has to be packed. Orders marked ready are usually packed would say something weaker, because usually leaves room for an exception. Our rule doesn\'t.",\r\n      "Now we get a fact: Order A is marked ready. What do you know about A? Take a moment to explain your answer before reading on. A has been packed. The rule covers every order marked ready, and the fact puts A in that group. A ready order that hadn\'t been packed would break the rule.",\r\n      "Keep the rule and change the fact: Order B has been packed. Must B be marked ready? Try to explain why before reading on. We don\'t know. The rule told us about ready orders. It didn\'t say every packed order is marked ready. B could have the ready label or still be waiting for it; either possibility fits what we\'ve been told.",\r\n      "We can now shorten the relationship to Marked ready → Packed. Read that as: if an order is marked ready, it has been packed. The arrow records the connection we\'ve already understood. It doesn\'t add a cause, a time sequence, or a fact about a particular order.",\r\n      "In the practice, separate the rule from the fact about the case. Then ask what that fact lets you conclude. Write the reason as well as the answer. That gives you something specific to compare with the explanation if your choice goes wrong.",\r\n    ],\r\n    method: [\r\n      "Read the rule in ordinary English: what does it say has to be true if the first condition is true?",\r\n      "Check the strength. Every ready order is packed gives a guarantee; usually packed allows an exception.",\r\n      "Find the separate fact about the case. A rule alone doesn\'t identify a ready order.",\r\n      "Compare the whole fact with the condition the rule covers. Order A is marked ready meets our ready-order condition.",\r\n      "State what follows and why. If the information leaves a question open, show how both possibilities could fit.",\r\n    ],\r\n    workedExample: {\r\n      statements: ["Every order marked ready has been packed.", "Order A is marked ready."],\r\n      reasoning: ["The rule tells us that every ready order has been packed. The fact identifies A as one of those orders.", "So A has been packed. We haven\'t assumed anything about a different order or about what made A ready."],\r\n      conclusion: "Order A has been packed.",\r\n    },\r\n    guidedExample: question("orientation-guide", "If a card is activated, its number is registered. Card L is activated. What follows?", ["Card L\'s number is registered.", "Every registered number belongs to an activated card.", "No other card is registered."], 0, "Card L is activated.
